WFNGC = Wide Flat Nose Gas Check
SnakeShot™
The #9 shot does not take the rifling in this shotshell, so there is not centrifugal spread, giving tighter 
patterns. Under the shot is a hardcast full wadcutter made to penetrate and cut into the intended 
target. This allows the SnakeShot™ to give a more dynamic result in the field while keeping recoil to 
very gentle levels!

Equalizer™
The Equalizer features a fully functioning JHP in the
front followed by a deep penetrating 21 brinell hardcast disk in
the back that has been gas checked. On impact the Equalizer
creates two distinct wound channels that group tightly
together out to 25 yards. When taking a shot to defend
yourself, two is always better than one.

Glock® Magazines - Bulk
Glock Magazines feature a two part construction with a stainless body with an
overmolded polymer wrap that protects the magazine from damage.
Witness holes in the back of the magazine are easy to read.
Glock magazines work in all generations of Glock pistols without issue and
are the best magazines available. Bulk - No packaging, ready to use.
